docker / homer

docs/corpus.md ribbon: ✅ The staged registry symptom was a missing CA/traced-store prerequisite; Alpine identity/layout and runtime-port interpolation deliberately dissolve · Evidence: runtime probe

Receipt status: Locked pnpm/Vite source build and HTTP app-mount probe pass · Full migration receipt

Pinned upstream source notes

Migration gaps

Generated: migrate.md@current · independently rechecked · 2026-08-05 · Status: current

  • The staged registry/CA symptom was not an irreducible pnpm wall: importing CA certificates, pinning pnpm's data tree inside FETCH, and installing offline lets the Vite build and HTTP check pass. → evidence
  • Docker's fixed lighttpd uid/gid, Alpine package layout, labels, and build-platform selector dissolve into the systemd identity and locked nixpkgs closure. → case
  • Native health checks use the declared default port 8080; changing Docker's interpolated PORT at runtime is not equivalent. → language

Upstream

Dockerfile

# build stage
FROM --platform=$BUILDPLATFORM node:24-alpine3.23 AS build-stage

ENV PNPM_HOME="/pnpm"
ENV PATH="$PNPM_HOME:$PATH"

RUN corepack enable && corepack use pnpm@10

WORKDIR /app

COPY package.json pnpm-lock.yaml pnpm-workspace.yaml ./
RUN pnpm install --frozen-lockfile

COPY . .
RUN pnpm build

# production stage
FROM alpine:3.23

ARG VERSION_TAG=latest

LABEL \
    org.label-schema.schema-version="1.0" \
    org.label-schema.version="$VERSION_TAG" \
    org.opencontainers.image.title="Homer Image" \
    org.opencontainers.image.description="A dead simple static Home-Page for your server to keep your services on hand, from a simple yaml configuration file." \
    org.opencontainers.image.ref.name="b4bz/homer:${VERSION_TAG}" \
    org.opencontainers.image.version="$VERSION_TAG" \
    org.opencontainers.image.licenses="Apache-2.0 license" \
    org.opencontainers.image.source="https://github.com/bastienwirtz/homer" \
    org.opencontainers.image.url="https://hub.docker.com/r/b4bz/homer"

ENV GID=1000 \
    UID=1000 \
    PORT=8080 \
    SUBFOLDER="/_" \
    INIT_ASSETS=1 \
    IPV6_DISABLE=0

RUN addgroup -S lighttpd -g ${GID} && adduser -D -S -u ${UID} lighttpd lighttpd && \
    apk add -U --no-cache tzdata lighttpd

WORKDIR /www

COPY lighttpd.conf /lighttpd.conf
COPY lighttpd-ipv6.sh /etc/lighttpd/ipv6.sh
COPY entrypoint.sh /entrypoint.sh
COPY --from=build-stage --chown=${UID}:${GID} /app/dist /www/
COPY --from=build-stage --chown=${UID}:${GID} /app/dist/assets /www/default-assets

USER ${UID}:${GID}

HEALTHCHECK --start-period=10s --start-interval=1s --interval=30s --timeout=5s --retries=3 \
    CMD wget --no-verbose -Y off --tries=1 --spider http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/ || exit 1

EXPOSE ${PORT}

ENTRYPOINT ["/bin/sh", "/entrypoint.sh"]

composix

Cixfile

FROM github:NixOS/nixpkgs/nixos-unstable AS pkgs

BUILDER build
  IMPORT ${pkgs.bash} ${pkgs.nodejs_24} ${pkgs.pnpm} ${pkgs.cacert} ${pkgs.coreutils} ${pkgs.gnused} ${pkgs.glibc}
  COPY context/package.json .
  COPY context/pnpm-lock.yaml .
  COPY context/pnpm-workspace.yaml .
  ENV COREPACK_ENABLE_PROJECT_SPEC=0
  ENV npm_config_manage_package_manager_versions=false
  ENV CI=true
  ENV XDG_DATA_HOME=$PWD/.cache/data
  RUN node -e 'const fs = require("fs"); const packageJson = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync("package.json", "utf8")); delete packageJson.packageManager; fs.writeFileSync("package.json", JSON.stringify(packageJson) + String.fromCharCode(10));'
  FETCH pnpm fetch --frozen-lockfile
  COPY context/ .
  RUN node -e 'const fs = require("fs"); const packageJson = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync("package.json", "utf8")); delete packageJson.packageManager; fs.writeFileSync("package.json", JSON.stringify(packageJson) + String.fromCharCode(10));'
  RUN rm -rf node_modules && pnpm install --offline --frozen-lockfile && pnpm build
  RUN mkdir -p www && cp -a dist/. www/ && cp -a dist/assets www/default-assets

SERVICE homer
  IMPORT ${pkgs.bash} ${pkgs.coreutils} ${pkgs.lighttpd} ${pkgs.tzdata}
  COPY lighttpd.cix.conf /lighttpd.conf
  COPY context/lighttpd-ipv6.sh /etc/lighttpd/ipv6.sh
  COPY entrypoint.cix.sh /entrypoint.sh
  COPY ${build}/www /www
  ENV PORT=8080
  ENV SUBFOLDER=/_
  ENV INIT_ASSETS=1
  ENV IPV6_DISABLE=0
  PORT http = $PORT
  CONFIGDIR /www/assets
  START bash /entrypoint.sh
  READINESS http 127.0.0.1:8080/ IN 10s
  LIVENESS http 127.0.0.1:8080/ EVERY 30s

check.sh 
#!/bin/sh
set -eu

if [ "${1:-}" != cix ]; then
    echo "usage: $0 cix" >&2
    exit 2
fi

cix=${CIX:-../../../../target/debug/cix}
item=$($cix build .#homer)
unit=$(sudo -n "$cix" run --detach "$item")

cleanup() {
    sudo -n systemctl stop "$unit" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
}
trap cleanup EXIT INT TERM

url="http://127.0.0.1:8080/"
i=0
while [ "$i" -lt 30 ]; do
    if body=$(curl --fail --silent --show-error --max-time 2 "$url"); then
        printf '%s' "$body" | grep -F '<div id="app-mount"></div>' >/dev/null
        exit 0
    fi
    i=$((i + 1))
    sleep 1
done

echo "Homer did not serve its root page at $url" >&2
exit 1

entrypoint.cix.sh

#!/usr/bin/env bash

if [[ "${INIT_ASSETS}" == "1" ]] && [[ ! -f "/www/assets/config.yml" ]]; then
    echo "No configuration found, installing default config & assets"
    if [[ -w "/www/assets/" ]]; then
        cp -Rn /www/default-assets/. /www/assets/
        cp -n /www/default-assets/config.yml.dist /www/assets/config.yml
    else
        echo "Assets directory not writable, skipping default config install."
        echo "Check assets directory permissions & docker user or skip default assets install by setting the INIT_ASSETS env var to 0."
    fi
fi

echo "Starting webserver"
exec 3>&1
exec lighttpd -D -f /lighttpd.conf

lighttpd.cix.conf

include_shell "/bin/bash /etc/lighttpd/ipv6.sh"

server.port            = env.PORT
server.modules         = ( "mod_alias", "mod_accesslog" )
server.document-root   = "/www"
alias.url              = ( env.SUBFOLDER => "/www" )
server.indexfiles      = ("index.html")
server.follow-symlink  = "enable"
server.feature-flags  += ( "server.clock-jump-restart" => 0 )
server.max-request-field-size = 65535
accesslog.filename = "/dev/fd/3"

$HTTP["remote-ip"] == "127.0.0.1" { accesslog.filename = "" }
$HTTP["remote-ip"] == "[::1]" { accesslog.filename = "" }

# homer migration receipt

the pinned `6972395b62463d4eec8ec5a1b12e60e8d482088a` context.

`/nix/store/s9qyvvn9krr5sg134h57j23gn4wj7lyp-cix-item-homer`. The locked FETCH
and offline build completed with pnpm 11.18.0 and Vite produced `dist/`.

service answered on `127.0.0.1:8080/` with Homer’s app mount. The run emitted
the current D36 `PrivatePIDs` degradation warning; it did not change the probe
